forall([gss_, gsa_, gsb_], associated(gss_, gsa_, gsb_) iff gsa_ in carr(gss_) and gsb_ in carr(gss_) and forsome([gsw_], is-unit(gss_, gsw_) and gsb_ = (mul(gss_))(gsw_, gsa_)))

cluster-point

forall([s, f, x], cluster-point(s, f, x) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and x in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forall([m in nn], forsome([n in nn], m <= n and (dist(s))(f(n), x) < eps))))

col-equiv

col-step-row

forall([r, n, b, i], col-step-row(r, n, b, i) iff forall([csj_ in interval(1, n)], i < csj_ implies entry(b, i, csj_) = zero(r)) and (i in interval(1, n) implies (entry(b, i, i) = zero(r) or entry(b, i, i) = one(r)) and (entry(b, i, i) = one(r) implies forall([csj_ in interval(1, n)], not(csj_ = i) implies entry(b, i, csj_) = zero(r)))))

forall([s, f], converges(s, f) iff forsome([l], converges-to(s, f, l)))

converges-along

forall([s, g, b, p], converges-along(s, g, b, p) iff is-metric-space(s) and g in fun(nn, pts(s)) and p in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([i in nn], i in b implies n <= i implies (dist(s))(g(i), p) <= eps))))

converges-ptwise-to

forall([cvps_, cvpt_, cvpf_, cvpg_], converges-ptwise-to(cvps_, cvpt_, cvpf_, cvpg_) iff is-metric-space(cvps_) and is-metric-space(cvpt_) and cvpf_ in fun(nn, fun(pts(cvps_), pts(cvpt_))) and cvpg_ in fun(pts(cvps_), pts(cvpt_)) and forall([cvpx_ in pts(cvps_), cvpe_], pos-rr(cvpe_) implies forsome([cvpn_ in nn], forall([cvpk_ in nn], cvpn_ <= cvpk_ implies (dist(cvpt_))((cvpf_(cvpk_))(cvpx_), cvpg_(cvpx_)) <= cvpe_))))

converges-to

forall([s, f, l], converges-to(s, f, l) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and l in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([n_ in nn], n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(n_), l) <= eps))))

converges-uniformly

forall([s, seq, g], converges-uniformly(s, seq, g) iff is-metric-space(s) and seq in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and g in fun(pts(s), rr) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((seq(k))(x) - g(x)) < eps)))))

converges-uniformly-to

cr-and-elim

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-and-elim(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mcf_, mca_, mcb_], mcc_ = [mcg_, mcf_] and enc-and(mca_, mcb_) in mcg_ and mchs_ = [[ctx-add(ctx-add(mcg_, mca_), mcb_), mcf_]]))

cr-and-intro

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-and-intro(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mca_, mcb_], mcc_ = [mcg_, enc-and(mca_, mcb_)] and mchs_ = [[mcg_, mca_], [mcg_, mcb_]]))

cr-assumption

cr-forall-intro

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-forall-intro(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mcb_, mcy_], mcc_ = [mcg_, enc-all(mcb_)] and mcy_ in nn and not(mcy_ in union(ctx-fv(mcg_), expr-fv(mcb_))) and mchs_ = [[mcg_, expr-open(mcb_, 0, efvar(mcy_))]]))

cr-forsome-elim

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-forsome-elim(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mcf_, mcb_, mcy_], mcc_ = [mcg_, mcf_] and enc-ex(mcb_) in mcg_ and mcy_ in nn and not(mcy_ in union(ctx-fv(mcg_), expr-fv(mcf_))) and mchs_ = [[ctx-add(mcg_, expr-open(mcb_, 0, efvar(mcy_))), mcf_]]))

cr-forsome-intro

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-forsome-intro(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mcb_, mctm_], mcc_ = [mcg_, enc-ex(mcb_)] and mctm_ in clt and mchs_ = [[mcg_, expr-open(mcb_, 0, mctm_)], [mcg_, enc-eq(mctm_, mctm_)]]))

cr-functoid-beta-eq

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-functoid-beta-eq(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mchd_, mcl_, mcb_], mcc_ = [mcg_, enc-qeq(eapp(mchd_, mcl_), openl(mcb_, mcl_, length(mcl_)))] and fun-def(mchd_, length(mcl_), mcb_) and mchs_ = []))

cr-if-false

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], cr-if-false(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff forsome([mcg_, mcf_, mcp_, mca_, mcb_], mcc_ = [mcg_, mcf_] and mchs_ = [[mcg_, enc-not(mcp_)], [ctx-add(mcg_, enc-qeq(enc-if(mcp_, mca_, mcb_), mcb_)), mcf_]]))

cr-if-true

cr-iff-elim

cr-intersection-mem

cr-weakening

cseq-equiv

forall([m, f, g], cseq-equiv(m, f, g) iff converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), 0))

def-app

forall([mdt_, mdhs_, mdc_], def-app(mdt_, mdhs_, mdc_) iff mdc_ in seq-set and forsome([mdg_, mdf_, mdd_, mda_], mdc_ = [mdg_, enc-eq(enc-apply(mdf_, mda_), enc-apply(mdf_, mda_))] and mdhs_ = [[mdg_, enc-in(mdf_, enc-fun1(mdd_))], [mdg_, enc-in(mda_, mdd_)]]))

def-comp

def-fun-sep

def-in-closed

def-list

def-pred-hyp

def-strict-hyp

def-struct-hyp

def-total

forall([mdt_, mdhs_, mdc_], def-total(mdt_, mdhs_, mdc_) iff mdc_ in seq-set and forsome([mdg_, mdh_, mdl_], mdc_ = [mdg_, enc-eq(eapp(mdh_, mdl_), eapp(mdh_, mdl_))] and total-head(mdh_) and length(mdhs_) = length(mdl_) and forall([mdi_ in nn], 1 <= mdi_ implies mdi_ <= length(mdl_) implies nth(mdi_, mdhs_) = [mdg_, enc-eq(nth(mdi_, mdl_), nth(mdi_, mdl_))])))

deg-bound

forall([a, p, n], deg-bound(a, p, n) iff forall([k_ in nn], n < k_ implies p(k_) = zero(a)))

divides

forall([gds_, gda_, gdb_], divides(gds_, gda_, gdb_) iff gda_ in carr(gds_) and gdb_ in carr(gds_) and forsome([gdk_ in carr(gds_)], gdb_ = (mul(gds_))(gdk_, gda_)))

econverges-to

forall([f, l], econverges-to(f, l) iff f in fun(nn, rr-pos-star) and l in rr-pos-star and eliminf(f) = l and elimsup(f) = l)

elem-equiv

forall([r, m, n, a, b], elem-equiv(r, m, n, a, b) iff forsome([eqp_, eqq_], eqp_ in elem-gen(r, m) and eqq_ in elem-gen(r, n) and b = matmul(r, matmul(r, eqp_, a), eqq_)))

eqv-ok

even

forall([x, l], ext-series-converges-to(x, l) iff l in rr-star and (l in rr implies forall([ece_], pos-rr(ece_) implies forsome([ecm_ in nn], forall([ecn_ in nn], ecm_ <= ecn_ implies ext-partial-sum(x, ecn_) in rr and abs(ext-partial-sum(x, ecn_) - l) < ece_)))) and (l = pos-inf implies forall([ecb_ in rr], forsome([ecm_ in nn], forall([ecn_ in nn], ecm_ <= ecn_ implies ecb_ < ext-partial-sum(x, ecn_))))) and (l = neg-inf implies forall([ecb_ in rr], forsome([ecm_ in nn], forall([ecn_ in nn], ecm_ <= ecn_ implies ext-partial-sum(x, ecn_) < ecb_)))))

is-annihilating-poly

is-antisymmetric-op

is-associative

forall([op, crr], is-associative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], op(op(u, v), w) = op(u, op(v, w))))

is-atom

is-bk-sequence

forall([bss_, bsg_, bsv_], is-bk-sequence(bss_, bsg_, bsv_) iff bsv_ in fun(nn, opens(bss_)) and bsv_(0) = pts(bss_) and forall([bsk_ in nn], iden(bsg_) in bsv_(bsk_) and forall([bsp_ in bsv_(bsk_)], (inv(bsg_))(bsp_) in bsv_(bsk_)) and forall([bsp_ in bsv_(succ(bsk_)), bsq_ in bsv_(succ(bsk_)), bsr_ in bsv_(succ(bsk_))], (opr(bsg_))((opr(bsg_))(bsp_, bsq_), bsr_) in bsv_(bsk_))))

is-bounded-l1-functional

forall([omega, ca, mu, f, cb], is-bounded-l1-functional(omega, ca, mu, f, cb) iff f in fun(l1-set(omega, ca, mu), rr) and cb in rr and 0 <= cb and forall([bfx_ in l1-set(omega, ca, mu), bfy_ in l1-set(omega, ca, mu)], f(vnb-lambda(sx_, omega, bfx_(sx_) + bfy_(sx_))) = f(bfx_) + f(bfy_)) and forall([bfr_ in rr, bfx_ in l1-set(omega, ca, mu)], f(vnb-lambda(sx_, omega, bfr_ * bfx_(sx_))) = bfr_ * f(bfx_)) and forall([bfx_ in l1-set(omega, ca, mu)], abs(f(bfx_)) <= cb * integral(omega, ca, mu, abs-fn(omega, bfx_))))

is-bounded-linear-functional

forall([m, f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) iff is-linear-functional(m, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))
