is-bounded-metric-space

forall([s], is-bounded-metric-space(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([b in rr], forall([x, y], x in pts(s) implies y in pts(s) implies (dist(s))(x, y) <= b)))

is-boundedly-equivalent

forall([phi, psi], is-boundedly-equivalent(phi, psi) iff phi in fun(nnreal, nnreal) and psi in fun(nnreal, nnreal) and forsome([bel_, beu_], pos-rr(bel_) and pos-rr(beu_) and forall([ber_ in rr], 0 < ber_ implies bel_ * phi(ber_) <= psi(ber_) and psi(ber_) <= beu_ * phi(ber_))))

is-caratheodory-measurable

forall([omega, mu, es], is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es) iff es subset omega and forall([a_ in power(omega)], mu(a_) = eplus(mu(intersection(a_, es)), mu(difference(a_, es)))))

is-cauchy-net

forall([m, t, d, x], is-cauchy-net(m, t, d, x) iff is-directed-set(d) and x in fun(pts(d), vec(m)) and forall([cnu_ in opens(t)], vzero(m) in cnu_ implies forsome([cni_ in pts(d)], forall([cnj_ in pts(d)], [cni_, cnj_] in dleq(d) implies forall([cnk_ in pts(d)], [cni_, cnk_] in dleq(d) implies (vadd(m))(x(cnj_), (vneg(m))(x(cnk_))) in cnu_)))))

is-cauchy-seq

forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], n <= m and n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= eps))))

is-cc-diff-at

forall([g, a, l], is-cc-diff-at(g, a, l) iff g in fun(rr, cc) and a in rr and l in cc and is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(sv_, rr, real-part(g(sv_))), a, real-part(l)) and is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(sv_, rr, imag-part(g(sv_))), a, imag-part(l)))

is-cc-linear-functional

forall([v, f], is-cc-linear-functional(v, f) iff f in fun(vec(v), cc) and forall([hlx_ in vec(v), hly_ in vec(v)], f((vadd(v))(hlx_, hly_)) = f(hlx_) + f(hly_)) and forall([hlc_ in cc, hlx_ in vec(v)], f((act(v))(hlc_, hlx_)) = hlc_ * f(hlx_)))

is-cc-module-over

forall([aa, vv], is-cc-module-over(aa, vv) iff vv subset cc and 0 in vv and forall([amx_ in vv, amy_ in vv], amx_ + amy_ in vv) and forall([amx_ in vv], -amx_ in vv) and forall([ama_ in aa, amx_ in vv], ama_ * amx_ in vv))

is-cc-space-dim-le

forall([kk, vv, n], is-cc-space-dim-le(kk, vv, n) iff is-cc-module-over(kk, vv) and forsome([amn_ in nn], amn_ <= n and forsome([amg_ in fun(interval(1, amn_), vv)], forall([amv_ in vv], forsome([amb_ in fun(interval(1, amn_), kk)], amv_ = cc-lincomb(amn_, amb_, amg_))))))

is-cc-subring

forall([kk], is-cc-subring(kk) iff kk subset cc and 0 in kk and 1 in kk and forall([psu_ in kk, psv_ in kk], psu_ + psv_ in kk) and forall([psu_ in kk, psv_ in kk], psu_ * psv_ in kk))

is-chain

forall([grd, porel, ch], is-chain(grd, porel, ch) iff ch subset grd and forall([x in ch, y in ch], [x, y] in porel or [y, x] in porel))

is-closed

forall([s, a], is-closed(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and is-open(s, complement-in(pts(s), a)))

is-cofinal-monotone

forall([e, d, h], is-cofinal-monotone(e, d, h) iff h in fun(pts(e), pts(d)) and forall([cmi_ in pts(e), cmj_ in pts(e)], [cmi_, cmj_] in dleq(e) implies [h(cmi_), h(cmj_)] in dleq(d)) and forall([cmk_ in pts(d)], forsome([cml_ in pts(e)], [cmk_, h(cml_)] in dleq(d))))

is-column-step

forall([r, m, n, b], is-column-step(r, m, n, b) iff forall([csi_ in interval(1, m), csj_ in interval(1, n)], csi_ < csj_ implies entry(b, csi_, csj_) = zero(r)) and forall([csi_ in interval(1, m)], csi_ in interval(1, n) implies entry(b, csi_, csi_) = zero(r) or entry(b, csi_, csi_) = one(r)))

is-commutative

forall([op, crr], is-commutative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr], op(u, v) = op(v, u)))

is-compact

forall([s], is-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([c], is-open-cover(s, c) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f))))

is-compact-exhaustion

forall([ces_, cek_], is-compact-exhaustion(ces_, cek_) iff is-metric-space(ces_) and cek_ in fun(nn, power(pts(ces_))) and forall([cen_ in nn], is-compact(subspace-ms(ces_, cek_(cen_)))) and forall([cen_ in nn], forsome([cer_], pos-rr(cer_) and forall([cey_ in cek_(cen_)], ball(ces_, cey_, cer_) subset cek_(cen_ + 1)))) and forall([cex_ in pts(ces_)], forsome([cen_ in nn], cex_ in cek_(cen_))) and forall([cea_], cea_ subset pts(ces_) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(ces_, cea_)) implies forsome([cen_ in nn], cea_ subset cek_(cen_))))

is-compact-nv-domain

forall([cwk_, cwa_], is-compact-nv-domain(cwk_, cwa_) iff is-nv-domain(cwk_, cwa_) and is-compact(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cwk_), cwa_)))

is-compact-t

forall([s], is-compact-t(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([c], forall([u in c], u in opens(s)) implies big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and big-union(u, f, u) == pts(s))))

is-complete

forall([s], is-complete(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies converges(s, f)))

is-complete-measure

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-complete-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-measure(omega, ca, mu) and forall([nls_], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, nls_) implies nls_ in ca))

is-complete-subset

forall([m, t, a], is-complete-subset(m, t, a) iff a subset vec(m) and forall([csd_, csx_], is-cauchy-net(m, t, csd_, csx_) implies forall([csi_ in pts(csd_)], csx_(csi_) in a) implies forsome([csl_ in a], net-converges-to(t, csd_, csx_, csl_))))

is-complex-measurable-fn

forall([omega, ca, u], is-complex-measurable-fn(omega, ca, u) iff u in fun(omega, cc) and is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, vnb-lambda(crx_, omega, real-part(u(crx_)))) and is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, vnb-lambda(crx_, omega, imag-part(u(crx_)))))

is-complex-measure

forall([omega, ca, nu], is-complex-measure(omega, ca, nu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and nu in fun(ca, cc) and forsome([cm1_, cm2_, cm3_, cm4_], is-finite-measure(omega, ca, cm1_) and is-finite-measure(omega, ca, cm2_) and is-finite-measure(omega, ca, cm3_) and is-finite-measure(omega, ca, cm4_) and forall([cma_ in ca], nu(cma_) = cm1_(cma_) - cm2_(cma_) + 1i * (cm3_(cma_) - cm4_(cma_)))))

is-complex-null-set

forall([omega, ca, nu, a], is-complex-null-set(omega, ca, nu, a) iff a in ca and forall([cnb_ in ca], cnb_ subset a implies nu(cnb_) = 0))

is-concat-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-concat-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sal_ in tuples(msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_)))], length(sas_) = succ(length(sal_)) and forall([sai_ in nn], 1 <= sai_ implies sai_ <= length(sal_) implies nth(sai_, sas_) = [received, nth(sai_, sal_)]) and nth(succ(length(sal_)), sas_) = [sent, lst(sal_)]))

is-connected

forall([cns_, cna_], is-connected(cns_, cna_) iff is-metric-space(cns_) and cna_ subset pts(cns_) and forall([cnu_], is-open(cns_, cnu_) implies forall([cnv_], is-open(cns_, cnv_) implies cna_ subset union(cnu_, cnv_) implies forall([cnx_ in cna_], not(cnx_ in cnu_ and cnx_ in cnv_)) implies forsome([cnx_ in cna_], cnx_ in cnu_) implies not(forsome([cnx_ in cna_], cnx_ in cnv_)))))

is-connected-t

forall([cts_, cta_], is-connected-t(cts_, cta_) iff cta_ subset pts(cts_) and forall([ctu_ in opens(cts_), ctv_ in opens(cts_)], cta_ subset union(ctu_, ctv_) implies forall([ctx_ in cta_], not(ctx_ in ctu_ and ctx_ in ctv_)) implies forsome([ctx_ in cta_], ctx_ in ctu_) implies not(forsome([ctx_ in cta_], ctx_ in ctv_))))

is-cont-lin

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), lim in vec(m1)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, lim) implies fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), tt(lim))))

is-continuous

forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([a in pts(s)], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a)))

is-continuous-at

forall([s, t, f, a], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and a in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))

is-continuous-on

forall([f, d], is-continuous-on(f, d) iff f in fun(d, rr) and forall([icp_ in d], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, d), rr-ms, f, icp_)))

is-conull

forall([omega, ca, mu, cs], is-conull(omega, ca, mu, cs) iff cs in ca and mu(complement-in(omega, cs)) = 0)

is-convex-cc

forall([cvu_], is-convex-cc(cvu_) iff cvu_ subset cc and forall([cvx_ in cvu_, cvy_ in cvu_, cvt_ in ccint(0, 1)], cvx_ + cvt_ * (cvy_ - cvx_) in cvu_))

is-convex-functional

forall([m, p], is-convex-functional(m, p) iff p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([cfx_ in vec(m)], 0 <= p(cfx_)) and forall([cfx_ in vec(m), cfy_ in vec(m), cft_ in rr], 0 <= cft_ implies cft_ <= 1 implies p((vadd(m))((act(m))(cft_, cfx_), (act(m))(1 - cft_, cfy_))) <= cft_ * p(cfx_) + (1 - cft_) * p(cfy_)))

is-convex-rr

forall([cvra_], is-convex-rr(cvra_) iff cvra_ subset rr and forall([cvrx_ in cvra_, cvry_ in cvra_, cvrt_ in ccint(0, 1)], cvrx_ + cvrt_ * (cvry_ - cvrx_) in cvra_))

is-convex-subset

forall([v, cs], is-convex-subset(v, cs) iff cs subset vec(v) and forall([sxa_ in cs, sxb_ in cs, sxt_ in rr], 0 <= sxt_ implies sxt_ <= 1 implies (vadd(v))((act(v))(1 - sxt_, sxa_), (act(v))(sxt_, sxb_)) in cs))

is-countable

forall([rpc_], is-countable(rpc_) iff rpc_ in set and (rpc_ = empty-set or forsome([rpen_ in fun(nn, rpc_)], forall([rpy_ in rpc_], forsome([rpn_ in nn], rpy_ = rpen_(rpn_))))))

is-countable-pseudometric-family

forall([fam, ground], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, ground) iff forall([n_ in nn], is-pseudometric-space(fam(n_)) and pts(fam(n_)) == ground))

is-countably-separated

forall([ys, cb], is-countably-separated(ys, cb) iff is-sigma-algebra(ys, cb) and forsome([csf_ in fun(nn, cb)], forall([csx_ in ys, csy_ in ys], not(csx_ = csy_) implies forsome([csk_ in nn], csx_ in csf_(csk_) and not(csy_ in csf_(csk_)) or csy_ in csf_(csk_) and not(csx_ in csf_(csk_))))))

is-cyclic-vector

forall([gcv-md_, gcv-t_, gcv-v_], is-cyclic-vector(gcv-md_, gcv-t_, gcv-v_) iff gcv-v_ in vec(gcv-md_) and forall([gcvw_ in vec(gcv-md_)], forsome([gcvq_ in carr(poly(scal(gcv-md_)))], gcvw_ = poly-op(gcv-md_, gcv-t_, gcvq_, gcv-v_))))

is-decrypt-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-decrypt-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sak_ in keys(sasp_), sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, (kinv(sasp_))(sak_)], [received, encr(sag_, sak_)], [sent, sag_]]))

is-degree-function

forall([gfs_, gfn_], is-degree-function(gfs_, gfn_) iff is-integral-domain(gfs_) and gfn_ in fun(carr(gfs_), nn) and forall([gfa_ in carr(gfs_), gfb_ in carr(gfs_)], not(gfa_ = zero(gfs_)) implies not(gfb_ = zero(gfs_)) implies gfn_(gfa_) <= gfn_((mul(gfs_))(gfa_, gfb_))) and forall([gfa_ in carr(gfs_), gfb_ in carr(gfs_)], not(gfa_ = zero(gfs_)) implies forsome([gfq_ in carr(gfs_)], gfb_ = (mul(gfs_))(gfa_, gfq_) or gfn_((add(gfs_))(gfb_, (neg(gfs_))((mul(gfs_))(gfa_, gfq_)))) < gfn_(gfa_))))

is-dense

forall([dns_, dna_], is-dense(dns_, dna_) iff is-metric-space(dns_) and dna_ subset pts(dns_) and pts(dns_) subset closure(dns_, dna_))

is-dense-seq

forall([s, dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([m in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(m)) < eps)))

is-diagonal

forall([a, m, n, d], is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) iff forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(i = j) implies entry(d, i, j) = zero(a)))

is-diff-at

forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and l in rr and forsome([phi in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in rr], f(x) - f(a) = phi(x) * (x - a))))

is-diff-at-v

forall([m, f, a, l], is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l) iff is-normed-vector-space(m) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and l in vec(m) and forsome([phi in fun(rr, vec(m))], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x_ in rr], (vadd(m))(f(x_), (vneg(m))(f(a))) = (act(m))(x_ - a, phi(x_)))))

is-diff-on

forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) iff is-normed-field(k) and is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) and f in fun(u, carr(k)) and a in u and l in carr(k) and forsome([phi in fun(u, carr(k))], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(k), u), nf-metric-space(k), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in u], (add(k))(f(x), (neg(k))(f(a))) = (mul(k))(phi(x), (add(k))(x, (neg(k))(a))))))

is-dir-diff-at

forall([m, f, a, eta, dl], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) iff is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), 0, dl))

is-discrete-ms

forall([dms_], is-discrete-ms(dms_) iff forall([dmx_ in pts(dms_)], forsome([dmr_], pos-rr(dmr_) and forall([dmy_ in ball(dms_, dmx_, dmr_)], dmy_ = dmx_))))

is-distributive

forall([addop, mulop, crr], is-distributive(addop, mulop, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], mulop(u, addop(v, w)) = addop(mulop(u, v), mulop(u, w)) and mulop(addop(u, v), w) = addop(mulop(u, w), mulop(v, w))))

is-doubling

forall([mhf_], is-doubling(mhf_) iff mhf_ in fun(nnreal, nnreal) and mhf_(0) = 0 and forall([mhr_ in nnreal, mhq_ in nnreal], mhr_ <= mhq_ implies mhf_(mhr_) <= mhf_(mhq_)) and forsome([mhp_], pos-rr(mhp_) and forall([mhr_ in rr], 0 < mhr_ implies mhf_(2 * mhr_) <= mhp_ * mhf_(mhr_))))

is-dynkin-system

forall([omega, dd], is-dynkin-system(omega, dd) iff omega in set and dd subset power(omega) and omega in dd and forall([dya_ in dd, dyb_ in dd], dya_ subset dyb_ implies complement-in(dyb_, dya_) in dd) and forall([dyf_ in fun(nn, dd)], forall([dyn_ in nn], dyf_(dyn_) subset dyf_(succ(dyn_))) implies big-union(dyk_, nn, dyf_(dyk_)) in dd))

is-element-order

forall([g, x, d], is-element-order(g, x, d) iff d in nn and 1 <= d and gpow(g, x, d) = iden(g) and forall([eod_ in nn], 1 <= eod_ implies gpow(g, x, eod_) = iden(g) implies d <= eod_))

is-elementary

forall([r, n, e], is-elementary(r, n, e) iff forsome([elk_, ell_], elk_ in interval(1, n) and ell_ in interval(1, n) and not(elk_ = ell_) and e = elem-f(r, n, elk_, ell_)) or forsome([elr_, elk_, ell_], elr_ in carr(r) and elk_ in interval(1, n) and ell_ in interval(1, n) and not(elk_ = ell_) and e = elem-g(r, n, elr_, elk_, ell_)) or forsome([elr_, elk_], elr_ in carr(r) and forsome([els_ in carr(r)], (mul(r))(elr_, els_) = one(r) and (mul(r))(els_, elr_) = one(r)) and elk_ in interval(1, n) and e = elem-h(r, n, elr_, elk_)))

is-encrypt-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-encrypt-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sak_ in keys(sasp_), sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sak_], [received, sag_], [sent, encr(sag_, sak_)]]))

is-entry-point

forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssi_], is-entry-point(ssp_, ssn_, ssi_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) and node-content(ssp_, ssn_) in ssi_ and forall([ssm_], same-strand-before(ssp_, ssm_, ssn_) implies not(node-content(ssp_, ssm_) in ssi_)))

is-eps-cauchy-seq

forall([s, eps, y], is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, y) iff is-metric-space(s) and y in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], (dist(s))(y(m), y(n_)) <= eps))

is-equicontinuous

forall([s, t, fam], is-equicontinuous(s, t, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), pts(t))) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([del], pos-rr(del) and forall([k in nn, y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) < del implies (dist(t))((fam(k))(x), (fam(k))(y)) < eps))))

is-equicontinuous-set

forall([eqss_, eqst_, eqse_], is-equicontinuous-set(eqss_, eqst_, eqse_) iff is-metric-space(eqss_) and is-metric-space(eqst_) and eqse_ subset fun(pts(eqss_), pts(eqst_)) and forall([eqsx_ in pts(eqss_), eqsp_], pos-rr(eqsp_) implies forsome([eqsd_], pos-rr(eqsd_) and forall([eqsf_ in eqse_, eqsy_ in pts(eqss_)], (dist(eqss_))(eqsx_, eqsy_) < eqsd_ implies (dist(eqst_))(eqsf_(eqsx_), eqsf_(eqsy_)) < eqsp_))))

is-equicontinuous-t

forall([t, ee], is-equicontinuous-t(t, ee) iff ee subset fun(pts(t), rr) and forall([eqx_ in pts(t), eqe_], pos-rr(eqe_) implies forsome([eqv_ in opens(t)], eqx_ in eqv_ and forall([eqf_ in ee, eqy_ in eqv_], abs(eqf_(eqx_) - eqf_(eqy_)) < eqe_))))

is-equivalence

forall([rho, crr], is-equivalence(rho, crr) iff rho in power(cartesian(crr, crr)) and forall([u in crr], [u, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr], [u, v] in rho implies [v, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], [u, v] in rho and [v, w] in rho implies [u, w] in rho))

is-essential-singularity

forall([mrf_, mra_], is-essential-singularity(mrf_, mra_) iff mra_ in cc and not(is-removable-singularity(mrf_, mra_)) and not(forsome([mrn_], is-pole(mrf_, mra_, mrn_))))

is-euclidean-domain

forall([ges_], is-euclidean-domain(ges_) iff is-integral-domain(ges_) and forsome([gen_], is-degree-function(ges_, gen_)))

is-eventually-in

forall([d, x, u], is-eventually-in(d, x, u) iff forsome([evi_ in pts(d)], forall([evj_ in pts(d)], [evi_, evj_] in dleq(d) implies x(evj_) in u)))

is-extremal-subset

forall([v, b, a], is-extremal-subset(v, b, a) iff is-convex-subset(v, b) and forsome([exz_], exz_ in b) and b subset a and forall([exx_, exy_, ext_], exx_ in a implies exy_ in a implies ext_ in rr implies 0 < ext_ implies ext_ < 1 implies (vadd(v))((act(v))(ext_, exx_), (act(v))(1 - ext_, exy_)) in b implies exx_ in b and exy_ in b))

is-extreme-point

forall([epm_, epk_, epx_], is-extreme-point(epm_, epk_, epx_) iff epx_ in epk_ and forall([epu_ in epk_, epv_ in epk_, ept_ in rr], 0 < ept_ implies ept_ < 1 implies epx_ = (vadd(epm_))((act(epm_))(1 - ept_, epu_), (act(epm_))(ept_, epv_)) implies epu_ = epx_ and epv_ = epx_))

is-fg-cc-module-over

forall([aa, vv], is-fg-cc-module-over(aa, vv) iff is-cc-module-over(aa, vv) and forsome([amn_ in nn, amg_ in fun(interval(1, amn_), vv)], forall([amv_ in vv], forsome([amb_ in fun(interval(1, amn_), aa)], amv_ = cc-lincomb(amn_, amb_, amg_)))))

is-finite-cover

forall([c, a], is-finite-cover(c, a) iff c in set and card(c) in nn and a subset big-union(u, c, u))

is-finite-dimensional

forall([m], is-finite-dimensional(m) iff is-vector-space(m) and is-noetherian(m))

is-finite-measure

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-finite-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-measure(omega, ca, mu) and mu(omega) in rr)

is-flat-regular

forall([frp_], is-flat-regular(frp_) iff is-pk-space(frp_) and forall([frn_ in nodes(ispace(pk-inf(frp_)))], is-regular-node(pk-inf(frp_), frn_) implies forsome([frk_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(frp_)))], forsome([frx_ in {frd_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(frp_))): not(frd_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(frp_))))}], node-content(ispace(pk-inf(frp_)), frn_) = encr(frx_, frk_)) or forsome([frl_ in tuples({frd_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(frp_))): not(frd_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(frp_))))})], node-content(ispace(pk-inf(frp_)), frn_) = encr(lst(frl_), frk_)))))

is-flush-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-flush-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sag_]]))

is-fr-bounded

forall([m, fam, aa], is-fr-bounded(m, fam, aa) iff aa subset vec(m) and forall([k in nn], forsome([bd in rr], forall([y in aa], (fam(k))(y) <= bd))))

is-fr-equicont

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, ix, tf], is-fr-equicont(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, ix, tf) iff tf in fun(ix, fun(vec(m1), vec(m2))) and forall([k in nn], forsome([nb in nn, ck], pos-rr(ck) and forall([i in ix, x in vec(m1), r in rr], forall([j in nn], j <= nb implies (fam1(j))(x) <= r) implies (fam2(k))((tf(i))(x)) <= ck * r))))

is-fractions-of

forall([cwk_, cwa_], is-fractions-of(cwk_, cwa_) iff forall([cwx_ in carr(cwk_)], forsome([cwu_ in cwa_, cwv_ in cwa_], not(cwv_ = zero(cwk_)) and (mul(cwk_))(cwx_, cwv_) = cwu_)))

is-frechet-structure

forall([fld, m, fam], is-frechet-structure(fld, m, fam) iff is-seminorm-family(fld, m, fam) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m))], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) implies forsome([lim in vec(m)], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim))))

is-fubini-set

forall([xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu, cs], is-fubini-set(xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu, cs) iff is-measurable-fn(xs, ca, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, cs)) and is-measurable-fn(ys, cb, y-section-measure(xs, ys, mu, cs)) and integral(xs, ca, mu, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, cs)) = integral(ys, cb, nu, y-section-measure(xs, ys, mu, cs)))

is-gauge-countable

forall([s], is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([fam], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, pts(s)) and pseudo-gauge-top(fam, pts(s)) == s))

is-gcd-set

forall([ggs_, ggt_, ggd_], is-gcd-set(ggs_, ggt_, ggd_) iff ggd_ in carr(ggs_) and forall([ggx_ in ggt_], divides(ggs_, ggd_, ggx_)) and forall([gge_ in carr(ggs_)], forall([ggx_ in ggt_], divides(ggs_, gge_, ggx_)) implies divides(ggs_, gge_, ggd_)))

is-generator

forall([g, x], is-generator(g, x) iff x in carr(g) and forall([geny_ in carr(g)], forsome([genk_ in zz], geny_ = group-zpow(g, x, genk_))))

is-group-metric

forall([mgm_, mgg_], is-group-metric(mgm_, mgg_) iff is-metric-space(mgm_) and is-group(mgg_) and pts(mgm_) = carr(mgg_) and forall([mgu_ in carr(mgg_), mgv_ in carr(mgg_), mge_], pos-rr(mge_) implies forsome([mgd_], pos-rr(mgd_) and forall([mgp_ in carr(mgg_), mgq_ in carr(mgg_)], (dist(mgm_))(mgu_, mgp_) <= mgd_ implies (dist(mgm_))(mgv_, mgq_) <= mgd_ implies (dist(mgm_))((opr(mgg_))(mgu_, mgv_), (opr(mgg_))(mgp_, mgq_)) <= mge_))) and is-continuous(mgm_, mgm_, inv(mgg_)))

is-group-norm

forall([nm, op, invop, unit, crr], is-group-norm(nm, op, invop, unit, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([u in crr], 0 <= nm(u) and (nm(u) = 0 iff u = unit) and nm(invop(u)) = nm(u) and forall([v in crr], nm(op(u, v)) <= nm(u) + nm(v))))

is-group-segment

forall([gss_, gsg_, gsa_, gsp_], is-group-segment(gss_, gsg_, gsa_, gsp_) iff pos-rr(gsa_) and gsp_ in fun(ccint(0, gsa_), carr(gsg_)) and forall([gsr_ in ccint(0, gsa_), gsu_ in ccint(0, gsa_)], gsr_ + gsu_ <= gsa_ implies gsp_(gsr_ + gsu_) = (opr(gsg_))(gsp_(gsr_), gsp_(gsu_))) and forall([gst_ in ccint(0, gsa_), gsw_ in opens(gss_)], gsp_(gst_) in gsw_ implies forsome([gsd_], pos-rr(gsd_) and forall([gsv_ in ccint(0, gsa_)], abs(gsv_ - gst_) < gsd_ implies gsp_(gsv_) in gsw_))))

is-group-topology

forall([tgs_, tgg_], is-group-topology(tgs_, tgg_) iff is-top-space(tgs_) and is-group(tgg_) and pts(tgs_) = carr(tgg_) and forall([tga_ in carr(tgg_), tgb_ in carr(tgg_), tgw_ in opens(tgs_)], (opr(tgg_))(tga_, (inv(tgg_))(tgb_)) in tgw_ implies forsome([tgu_ in opens(tgs_)], tga_ in tgu_ and forsome([tgv_ in opens(tgs_)], tgb_ in tgv_ and forall([tgp_ in tgu_, tgq_ in tgv_], (opr(tgg_))(tgp_, (inv(tgg_))(tgq_)) in tgw_)))))

is-hausdorff

forall([s], is-hausdorff(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies not(a == b) implies forsome([u, v], u in opens(s) and v in opens(s) and a in u and b in v and intersection(u, v) == empty-set)))

is-hausdorff-dim

forall([mhm_, mhs_, mhl_], is-hausdorff-dim(mhm_, mhs_, mhl_) iff mhl_ in rr and 0 <= mhl_ and forall([mhr_ in rr], mhl_ < mhr_ implies (hausdorff(mhm_, mhr_))(mhs_) = 0) and forall([mhr_ in rr], 0 < mhr_ implies mhr_ < mhl_ implies (hausdorff(mhm_, mhr_))(mhs_) = pos-inf))

is-hereditary

forall([ca, cb], is-hereditary(ca, cb) iff cb subset ca and forall([hde_ in cb, hdf_ in ca], hdf_ subset hde_ implies hdf_ in cb))

is-hilbert-space

forall([v], is-hilbert-space(v) iff is-complex-inner-product-space(v) and is-complete(ip-metric-space(v)))

is-holomorphic-automorphism

forall([hau_, hap_], is-holomorphic-automorphism(hau_, hap_) iff hap_ in bijection(hau_, hau_) and holomorphic-on(hau_, hap_))

is-homeomorphism

forall([hmes_, hmet_, hmef_], is-homeomorphism(hmes_, hmet_, hmef_) iff hmef_ in bijection(pts(hmes_), pts(hmet_)) and is-continuous(hmes_, hmet_, hmef_) and is-continuous(hmet_, hmes_, inverse-bij(hmef_, pts(hmes_), pts(hmet_))))

is-honest

forall([shp_, shc_, shi_], is-honest(shp_, shc_, shi_) iff forall([shn_ in bnodes(shc_)], is-adversary-node(shp_, shn_) implies is-entry-point(ispace(shp_), shn_, shi_) implies is-text-trace(ispace(shp_), adv-tokens(shp_), (strand-map(ispace(shp_)))(nth(2, shn_)))))

is-ideal

forall([s, i], is-ideal(s, i) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and i subset carr(s) and zero(s) in i and forall([a in i, b in i], (add(s))(a, b) in i) and forall([a in i], (neg(s))(a) in i) and forall([r in carr(s), a in i], (mul(s))(r, a) in i))

is-ideal-in

forall([i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_], is-ideal-in(i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_) iff i_ subset carr_ and zero_ in i_ and forall([a, b], a in i_ implies b in i_ implies add_(a, b) in i_) and forall([a in i_], neg_(a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(x_, a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(a, x_) in i_))

is-identity

forall([op, unit, crr], is-identity(op, unit, crr) iff forall([u in crr], op(unit, u) = u and op(u, unit) = u))

is-init-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-init-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nlm_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [received, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_, nlb_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [sent, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-integrable

forall([omega, ca, mu, f], is-integrable(omega, ca, mu, f) iff is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and integral(omega, ca, mu, pos-part(omega, f)) in rr and integral(omega, ca, mu, neg-part(omega, f)) in rr)

is-interval

forall([es], is-interval(es) iff es subset rr and forall([mia_ in es, mib_ in es, mix_ in rr], mia_ <= mix_ implies mix_ <= mib_ implies mix_ in es))

is-invariant-factors

forall([l, a], is-invariant-factors(l, a) iff a in fun(interval(1, l), zz) and forall([ifi_ in interval(1, l)], 0 <= a(ifi_)) and forall([ifi_ in interval(1, l)], not(a(ifi_) = 1)) and forall([ifi_, ifj_], ifi_ in interval(1, l) implies ifj_ in interval(1, l) implies ifi_ <= ifj_ implies zz-divides(a(ifi_), a(ifj_))))

is-invertible-mat

forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) iff u in mat(n, n, carr(a)) and forsome([v in mat(n, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, u, v) = identmat(a, n) and matmul(a, v, u) = identmat(a, n)))

is-iso-group

forall([qag_, qah_, azk_], is-iso-group(qag_, qah_, azk_) iff is-hom-group(qag_, qah_, azk_) and azk_ in bijection(carr(qag_), carr(qah_)))

is-iso-module

forall([a, b, f], is-iso-module(a, b, f) iff is-hom-module(a, b, f) and f in bijection(vec(a), vec(b)))

is-iso-ring

forall([a, b, f], is-iso-ring(a, b, f) iff is-hom-ring(a, b, f) and f in bijection(carr(a), carr(b)))

is-isometric-op

forall([v, jo], is-isometric-op(v, jo) iff forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (rip(v))(jo(x_), jo(y_)) = (rip(v))(x_, y_)))

is-isometry

forall([s, t, f], is-isometry(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(t))(f(x), f(y)) = (dist(s))(x, y)))

is-k-linear

forall([m, f], is-k-linear(m, f) iff is-module(m) and f in fun(vec(m), carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))

is-k-linear-on

forall([m, s, f], is-k-linear-on(m, s, f) iff is-module(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and f in fun(s, carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in s, y in s], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in s], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))

is-key-ideal

forall([sbit_, sbik_, sbiks_, sbii_], is-key-ideal(sbit_, sbik_, sbiks_, sbii_) iff forall([sbix_ in sbii_], sbix_ in msg-set(sbit_, sbik_)) and forall([sbil_, sbij_], sbil_ in tuples(msg-set(sbit_, sbik_)) implies sbij_ in nn implies 1 <= sbij_ implies sbij_ <= length(sbil_) implies nth(sbij_, sbil_) in sbii_ implies lst(sbil_) in sbii_) and forall([sbih_, sbiy_], sbih_ in sbii_ implies sbiy_ in sbiks_ implies encr(sbih_, sbiy_) in sbii_))

is-left-invariant

forall([lim_, lig_], is-left-invariant(lim_, lig_) iff forall([lia_ in carr(lig_), lix_ in carr(lig_), liy_ in carr(lig_)], (dist(lim_))((opr(lig_))(lia_, lix_), (opr(lig_))(lia_, liy_)) = (dist(lim_))(lix_, liy_)))

is-left-limit

forall([f, x, l], is-left-limit(f, x, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and x in rr and l in rr and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([t_], t_ in rr and x - delta_ <= t_ and t_ < x implies abs(f(t_) - l) <= eps_))))

is-left-limit-within

forall([rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_], is-left-limit-within(rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_) iff rpw_ subset rr and rpf_ in fun(rpw_, rr) and rpx_ in rr and rpl_ in rr and forall([rple_], pos-rr(rple_) implies forsome([rpld_], pos-rr(rpld_) and forall([rplt_ in rpw_], rpx_ - rpld_ < rplt_ implies rplt_ < rpx_ implies abs(rpf_(rplt_) - rpl_) < rple_))))

is-limit-along

forall([lmas_, lmat_, lmaa_, lmaf_, lmax_, lmay_], is-limit-along(lmas_, lmat_, lmaa_, lmaf_, lmax_, lmay_) iff is-metric-space(lmas_) and is-metric-space(lmat_) and lmay_ in pts(lmat_) and forall([lmae_], pos-rr(lmae_) implies forsome([lmad_], pos-rr(lmad_) and forall([lmaz_ in lmaa_], (dist(lmas_))(lmax_, lmaz_) <= lmad_ implies (dist(lmat_))(lmaf_(lmaz_), lmay_) <= lmae_))))

is-linear-functional

forall([m, f], is-linear-functional(m, f) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))

is-linear-functional-on

forall([m, s, f], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff f in fun(s, rr) and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in s], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))

is-linear-map

forall([m1, m2, tt], is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) iff is-module(m1) and is-module(m2) and scal(m1) = scal(m2) and tt in fun(vec(m1), vec(m2)) and forall([x in vec(m1), y in vec(m1)], tt((vadd(m1))(x, y)) = (vadd(m2))(tt(x), tt(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m1)), x in vec(m1)], tt((act(m1))(lam, x)) = (act(m2))(lam, tt(x))))

is-local-hom

forall([lhs1_, lhg1_, lhs2_, lhg2_, lhv_, lhf_], is-local-hom(lhs1_, lhg1_, lhs2_, lhg2_, lhv_, lhf_) iff lhv_ in opens(lhs1_) and iden(lhg1_) in lhv_ and lhf_ in fun(lhv_, carr(lhg2_)) and forall([lhx_ in lhv_, lho_ in opens(lhs2_)], lhf_(lhx_) in lho_ implies forsome([lhu_ in opens(lhs1_)], lhx_ in lhu_ and forall([lhy_ in lhu_], lhy_ in lhv_ implies lhf_(lhy_) in lho_))) and forsome([lhw_ in opens(lhs1_)], iden(lhg1_) in lhw_ and forall([lhx_ in lhw_], (inv(lhg1_))(lhx_) in lhw_) and forall([lhx_ in lhw_, lhy_ in lhw_], (opr(lhg1_))(lhx_, lhy_) in lhv_ and lhf_((opr(lhg1_))(lhx_, lhy_)) = (opr(lhg2_))(lhf_(lhx_), lhf_(lhy_)))))

is-local-max-at

forall([f, d, lmt_], is-local-max-at(f, d, lmt_) iff f in fun(d, rr) and lmt_ in d and forsome([lma_], pos-rr(lma_) and forall([lmx_ in d], abs(lmx_ - lmt_) <= lma_ implies f(lmx_) <= f(lmt_))))

is-local-measure

forall([omega, ca, cb, rho], is-local-measure(omega, ca, cb, rho) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and is-hereditary(ca, cb) and rho in fun(cb, rr-pos-star) and forall([lme_ in cb], is-measure(lme_, restrict-sigma(ca, lme_), restrict(rho, restrict-sigma(ca, lme_)))))

is-local-min-at

forall([f, d, lmt_], is-local-min-at(f, d, lmt_) iff f in fun(d, rr) and lmt_ in d and forsome([lma_], pos-rr(lma_) and forall([lmx_ in d], abs(lmx_ - lmt_) <= lma_ implies f(lmt_) <= f(lmx_))))

is-local-nv-field

forall([lck_], is-local-nv-field(lck_) iff is-nv-field(lck_) and not(is-discrete-ms(nf-metric-space(lck_))) and is-locally-compact(nf-metric-space(lck_)))

is-locally-compact

forall([lcs_], is-locally-compact(lcs_) iff is-metric-space(lcs_) and forall([lca_ in pts(lcs_)], forsome([lcr_], pos-rr(lcr_) and is-compact(subspace-ms(lcs_, closed-ball(lcs_, lca_, lcr_))))))

is-locally-null

forall([ca, cb, rho, es], is-locally-null(ca, cb, rho, es) iff es in ca and forall([lna_ in cb], rho(intersection(lna_, es)) = 0))

is-lower-triangular

forall([a, n, d], is-lower-triangular(a, n, d) iff forall([ltu_, ltv_], ltu_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ in interval(1, n) implies ltu_ < ltv_ implies entry(d, ltu_, ltv_) = zero(a)))

is-lusin

forall([s, e], is-lusin(s, e) iff forsome([lzt_], is-complete(lzt_) and is-separable(lzt_) and forsome([lzh_], is-continuous(lzt_, subspace-ms(s, e), lzh_) and image(lzh_, pts(lzt_)) = e and forall([lzx_ in pts(lzt_), lzy_ in pts(lzt_)], lzh_(lzx_) = lzh_(lzy_) implies lzx_ = lzy_))))

is-matrix-of

forall([gmx-md_, gmx-n_, gmx-u_, gmx-t_, gmx-a_], is-matrix-of(gmx-md_, gmx-n_, gmx-u_, gmx-t_, gmx-a_) iff gmx-a_ in mat(gmx-n_, gmx-n_, carr(scal(gmx-md_))) and forall([gmxj_ in interval(1, gmx-n_)], gmx-t_(entry(gmx-u_, gmxj_, 1)) = lincomb(gmx-md_, gmx-n_, col-as-row(gmx-a_, gmx-n_, gmxj_), gmx-u_)))

is-maximal

forall([grd, porel, mx], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx) iff mx in grd and forall([y in grd], [mx, y] in porel implies [y, mx] in porel))

is-meager

forall([s, a], is-meager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and forsome([ee in fun(nn, power(pts(s)))], forall([n in nn], is-nowhere-dense(s, ee(n))) and a subset big-union(n, nn, ee(n))))

is-measurable-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and f in fun(omega, rr-pos-star) and forall([alpha_ in rr], {x_ in omega: alpha_ < f(x_)} in ca))

is-measurable-map

forall([omega, ca, omega2, cb, f], is-measurable-map(omega, ca, omega2, cb, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and is-sigma-algebra(omega2, cb) and f in fun(omega, omega2) and forall([b_ in cb], {x_ in omega: f(x_) in b_} in ca))

is-measurable-step-fn

forall([omega, ca, s], is-measurable-step-fn(omega, ca, s) iff is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, s) and card(image(s, omega)) in nn)

is-measure

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and mu in fun(ca, rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies not(i_ = j_) implies intersection(f_(i_), f_(j_)) = empty-set) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) = esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))

is-measure-isomorphism

forall([omega, ca, mu, omega2, cb, nu, tt], is-measure-isomorphism(omega, ca, mu, omega2, cb, nu, tt) iff is-measurable-map(omega, ca, omega2, cb, tt) and forall([mib_ in cb], nu(mib_) = mu({mix_ in omega: tt(mix_) in mib_})) and forsome([mis_], is-measurable-map(omega2, cb, omega, ca, mis_) and forall([mix_ in omega], mis_(tt(mix_)) = mix_) and forall([miy_ in omega2], tt(mis_(miy_)) = miy_)))

is-meromorphic-at

forall([mrf_, mra_], is-meromorphic-at(mrf_, mra_) iff mra_ in cc and forsome([mrn_ in nn, mrr_ in rr], 0 < mrr_ and forsome([mrg_], holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), mrg_) and forall([mrz_ in difference(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), singleton(mra_))], mrf_(mrz_) = recip(mrz_ - mra_) ^ mrn_ * mrg_(mrz_)))))

is-metric

forall([dst, crr], is-metric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and (dst(u, v) = 0 implies u = v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))

is-metric-outer-measure

forall([m, mu], is-metric-outer-measure(m, mu) iff is-metric-space(m) and is-outer-measure(pts(m), mu) and forall([moa_ in power(pts(m)), mob_ in power(pts(m))], not(set-dist(m, moa_, mob_) = 0) implies mu(union(moa_, mob_)) = eplus(mu(moa_), mu(mob_))))

is-minimal-poly

forall([gmp-md_, gmp-t_, gmp-k_], is-minimal-poly(gmp-md_, gmp-t_, gmp-k_) iff is-monic(scal(gmp-md_), gmp-k_) and forall([gmpw_ in vec(gmp-md_)], poly-op(gmp-md_, gmp-t_, gmp-k_, gmpw_) = vzero(gmp-md_)) and forall([gmpq_ in carr(poly(scal(gmp-md_)))], not(gmpq_ = zero(poly(scal(gmp-md_)))) implies forall([gmpw_ in vec(gmp-md_)], poly-op(gmp-md_, gmp-t_, gmpq_, gmpw_) = vzero(gmp-md_)) implies deg(scal(gmp-md_), gmp-k_) <= deg(scal(gmp-md_), gmpq_)))

is-monic

forall([gmo-f_, gmo-p_], is-monic(gmo-f_, gmo-p_) iff gmo-p_ in carr(poly(gmo-f_)) and gmo-p_(deg(gmo-f_, gmo-p_)) = one(gmo-f_))

is-monotone-class

forall([omega, mc], is-monotone-class(omega, mc) iff omega in set and mc subset power(omega) and forall([mcf_ in fun(nn, mc)], forall([mcn_ in nn], mcf_(mcn_) subset mcf_(succ(mcn_))) implies big-union(mck_, nn, mcf_(mck_)) in mc) and forall([mcg_ in fun(nn, mc)], forall([mcm_ in nn], mcg_(succ(mcm_)) subset mcg_(mcm_)) implies complement-in(omega, big-union(mcj_, nn, complement-in(omega, mcg_(mcj_)))) in mc))

is-ms-sequence

forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) iff forall([n in nn], is-metric-space(ms(n))))

is-multiplicative

forall([f], is-multiplicative(f) iff f in fun(nn, rr) and forall([mlm_ in nn, mln_ in nn], 1 <= mlm_ implies 1 <= mln_ implies zz-coprime(mlm_, mln_) implies f(mlm_ * mln_) = f(mlm_) * f(mln_)))

is-nilpotent-op

forall([gnp-md_, gnp-t_], is-nilpotent-op(gnp-md_, gnp-t_) iff forsome([gnpk_ in nn], forall([gnpw_ in vec(gnp-md_)], op-iter(gnp-md_, gnp-t_, gnpw_, gnpk_) = vzero(gnp-md_))))

is-noetherian

forall([m], is-noetherian(m) iff is-module(m) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(vec(m)))], forall([n_ in nn], is-submodule(m, f_(n_))) and forall([n_ in nn], f_(n_) subset f_(succ(n_))) implies forsome([k_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and k_ <= n_ implies f_(n_) = f_(k_)))))

is-nonmeager

forall([s, a], is-nonmeager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and not(is-meager(s, a)))

is-nontrivial-character

forall([chk_, chc_], is-nontrivial-character(chk_, chc_) iff is-add-character(chk_, chc_) and forsome([chx_ in carr(chk_)], not(chc_(chx_) = 1)))

is-nontrivial-nv

forall([cpk_], is-nontrivial-nv(cpk_) iff forsome([cpa_ in carr(cpk_)], not((fnrm(cpk_))(cpa_) = 0) and not((fnrm(cpk_))(cpa_) = 1)))

is-nontrivially-valued

forall([fld], is-nontrivially-valued(fld) iff is-normed-field(fld) and forsome([c in carr(fld)], 1 < (fnrm(fld))(c) and forsome([b in carr(fld)], (mul(fld))(c, b) = one(fld))))

is-norm

forall([nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr], is-norm(nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([a in crr], 0 <= nm(a) and (nm(a) = 0 iff a = zr) and forall([b in crr], nm(mulop(a, b)) = nm(a) * nm(b) and nm(addop(a, b)) <= nm(a) + nm(b))))

is-norm-data

forall([md, sm, n, xs, ys, am], is-norm-data(md, sm, n, xs, ys, am) iff n in nn and xs in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) and generates(md, n, xs) and rel-free(md, n, xs) and ys in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) and spans(md, n, ys, sm) and rel-free(md, n, ys) and am in mat(n, n, zz) and ys = matact(md, am, xs))

is-normal-subgroup

forall([sgg_, sgh_], is-normal-subgroup(sgg_, sgh_) iff is-subgroup(sgg_, sgh_) and forall([sgu_ in carr(sgg_), sga_ in sgh_], (opr(sgg_))((opr(sgg_))(sgu_, sga_), (inv(sgg_))(sgu_)) in sgh_))

is-normally-convergent

forall([u, cpsxs_], is-normally-convergent(u, cpsxs_) iff forall([cpsi_ in nn], cc-sup-norm(u(cpsi_), cpsxs_) in rr) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsi_, nn, cc-sup-norm(u(cpsi_), cpsxs_))))

is-nowhere-dense

forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and interior(s, closure(s, a)) = empty-set)

is-nsl-space

forall([nlp_], is-nsl-space(nlp_) iff is-pk-space(nlp_) and forall([nli_ in ids(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))], nli_ in adv-ids(pk-inf(nlp_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-init-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-resp-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_))))

is-nso-init-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-nso-init-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nlm_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [received, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [sent, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-nso-resp-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-nso-resp-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nln_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[received, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [received, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-nso-space

forall([nlp_], is-nso-space(nlp_) iff is-pk-space(nlp_) and forall([nli_ in ids(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))], nli_ in adv-ids(pk-inf(nlp_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-nso-init-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-nso-resp-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_))))

is-null-set

forall([omega, ca, mu, ns], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, ns) iff forsome([nla_ in ca], ns subset nla_ and mu(nla_) = 0))

is-nv-content

forall([cw3k_, cw3p_, cw3c_], is-nv-content(cw3k_, cw3p_, cw3c_) iff forall([cw3i_ in nn], (fnrm(cw3k_))(cw3p_(cw3i_)) <= cw3c_) and forsome([cw3i_ in nn], (fnrm(cw3k_))(cw3p_(cw3i_)) = cw3c_))

is-nv-content-one

forall([cw3k_, cw3p_], is-nv-content-one(cw3k_, cw3p_) iff forall([cw3i_ in nn], (fnrm(cw3k_))(cw3p_(cw3i_)) <= 1) and forsome([cw3i_ in nn], (fnrm(cw3k_))(cw3p_(cw3i_)) = 1))

is-nv-domain

forall([cwk_, cwa_], is-nv-domain(cwk_, cwa_) iff is-nv-field(cwk_) and forall([cwx_ in cwa_], cwx_ in carr(cwk_)) and zero(cwk_) in cwa_ and one(cwk_) in cwa_ and forall([cwx_ in cwa_, cwy_ in cwa_], (add(cwk_))(cwx_, cwy_) in cwa_) and forall([cwx_ in cwa_, cwy_ in cwa_], (mul(cwk_))(cwx_, cwy_) in cwa_) and forall([cwx_ in cwa_], (neg(cwk_))(cwx_) in cwa_) and forall([cwx_ in cwa_], (fnrm(cwk_))(cwx_) <= 1))

is-nv-field

forall([cwk_], is-nv-field(cwk_) iff is-normed-field(cwk_) and forall([cwx_ in carr(cwk_), cwy_ in carr(cwk_)], (fnrm(cwk_))((add(cwk_))(cwx_, cwy_)) <= max((fnrm(cwk_))(cwx_), (fnrm(cwk_))(cwy_))))

is-nv-ideal

forall([cwk_, cwa_, cwi_], is-nv-ideal(cwk_, cwa_, cwi_) iff forall([cwx_ in cwi_], cwx_ in cwa_) and zero(cwk_) in cwi_ and forall([cwx_ in cwi_, cwy_ in cwi_], (add(cwk_))(cwx_, cwy_) in cwi_) and forall([cwx_ in cwi_], (neg(cwk_))(cwx_) in cwi_) and forall([cwr_ in cwa_, cwx_ in cwi_], (mul(cwk_))(cwr_, cwx_) in cwi_))

is-nv-module

forall([cw3k_, cw3a_, cw3e_], is-nv-module(cw3k_, cw3a_, cw3e_) iff forall([cw3x_ in cw3e_], cw3x_ in carr(cw3k_)) and zero(cw3k_) in cw3e_ and forall([cw3x_ in cw3e_, cw3y_ in cw3e_], (add(cw3k_))(cw3x_, cw3y_) in cw3e_) and forall([cw3x_ in cw3e_], (neg(cw3k_))(cw3x_) in cw3e_) and forall([cw3r_ in cw3a_, cw3x_ in cw3e_], (mul(cw3k_))(cw3r_, cw3x_) in cw3e_))

is-nv-pid

forall([cwk_, cwa_], is-nv-pid(cwk_, cwa_) iff is-nv-domain(cwk_, cwa_) and forall([cwi_], is-nv-ideal(cwk_, cwa_, cwi_) implies forsome([cwg_ in cwa_], cwi_ = nv-principal(cwk_, cwa_, cwg_))))

is-one-parameter-group

forall([ops_, opg_, opp_], is-one-parameter-group(ops_, opg_, opp_) iff opp_ in fun(rr, carr(opg_)) and forall([oqr_ in rr, oqu_ in rr], opp_(oqr_ + oqu_) = (opr(opg_))(opp_(oqr_), opp_(oqu_))) and forall([oqt_ in rr, oqw_ in opens(ops_)], opp_(oqt_) in oqw_ implies forsome([oqd_], pos-rr(oqd_) and forall([oqv_ in rr], abs(oqv_ - oqt_) < oqd_ implies opp_(oqv_) in oqw_))))

is-open

forall([s, u], is-open(s, u) iff is-metric-space(s) and u subset pts(s) and forall([y in u], forsome([r], pos-rr(r) and ball(s, y, r) subset u)))

is-open-cover

forall([s, c], is-open-cover(s, c) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([u in c], is-open(s, u)) and big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s))

is-open-gauge

forall([fam, ground, u], is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u) iff u subset ground and forall([y in u], forsome([k_ in nn, r], pos-rr(r) and forall([x in ground], forall([n_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies (dist(fam(n_)))(y, x) < r) implies x in u))))

is-open-lin-map

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-open-lin-map(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([n in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn, eps_], pos-rr(eps_) and fr-ball(m2, fam2, n_, eps_) subset image(tt, fr-ball(m1, fam1, n, eps)))))

is-or-init

forall([orip_, oris_, oria_, orib_, orin_, orim_, orik_], is-or-init(orip_, oris_, oria_, orib_, orin_, orim_, orik_) iff oria_ in sk-names(orip_) and orib_ in sk-names(orip_) and is-or-text(orip_, orin_) and is-or-text(orip_, orim_) and orik_ in keys(ispace(sk-inf(orip_))) and oris_ = [[sent, lst([orim_, oria_, orib_, encr(lst([orin_, orim_, oria_, orib_]), (ltkey(orip_))(oria_))])], [received, lst([orim_, encr(lst([orin_, orik_]), (ltkey(orip_))(oria_))])]])

is-or-resp

forall([orrp_, orrs_, orra_, orrb_, orrn_, orrm_, orrk_, orrh_, orrg_], is-or-resp(orrp_, orrs_, orra_, orrb_, orrn_, orrm_, orrk_, orrh_, orrg_) iff orra_ in sk-names(orrp_) and orrb_ in sk-names(orrp_) and is-or-text(orrp_, orrn_) and is-or-text(orrp_, orrm_) and orrk_ in keys(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_))) and orrh_ in msg-set(tokens(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_))), keys(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_)))) and orrg_ in msg-set(tokens(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_))), keys(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_)))) and not(orrn_ = orrm_) and not(is-subterm(tokens(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_))), keys(ispace(sk-inf(orrp_))), orrn_, orrh_)) and orrs_ = [[received, lst([orrm_, orra_, orrb_, orrh_])], [sent, lst([orrm_, orra_, orrb_, orrh_, encr(lst([orrn_, orrm_, orra_, orrb_]), (ltkey(orrp_))(orrb_))])], [received, lst([orrm_, orrg_, encr(lst([orrn_, orrk_]), (ltkey(orrp_))(orrb_))])], [sent, lst([orrm_, orrg_])]])

is-or-serv

forall([orsp_, orss_, orsa_, orsb_, orsn_, orso_, orsm_, orsk_], is-or-serv(orsp_, orss_, orsa_, orsb_, orsn_, orso_, orsm_, orsk_) iff orsa_ in sk-names(orsp_) and orsb_ in sk-names(orsp_) and is-or-text(orsp_, orsn_) and is-or-text(orsp_, orso_) and is-or-text(orsp_, orsm_) and orsk_ in keys(ispace(sk-inf(orsp_))) and not(orsk_ in adv-tokens(sk-inf(orsp_))) and forall([orsx_ in sk-names(orsp_)], not(orsk_ = (ltkey(orsp_))(orsx_))) and (kinv(ispace(sk-inf(orsp_))))(orsk_) = orsk_ and orss_ = [[received, lst([orsm_, orsa_, orsb_, encr(lst([orsn_, orsm_, orsa_, orsb_]), (ltkey(orsp_))(orsa_)), encr(lst([orso_, orsm_, orsa_, orsb_]), (ltkey(orsp_))(orsb_))])], [sent, lst([orsm_, encr(lst([orsn_, orsk_]), (ltkey(orsp_))(orsa_)), encr(lst([orso_, orsk_]), (ltkey(orsp_))(orsb_))])]])

is-or-space

forall([orqp_], is-or-space(orqp_) iff is-sk-space(orqp_) and forall([orqi_ in ids(ispace(sk-inf(orqp_)))], orqi_ in adv-ids(sk-inf(orqp_)) or forsome([orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqm_, orqk_], is-or-init(orqp_, (strand-map(ispace(sk-inf(orqp_))))(orqi_), orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqm_, orqk_)) or forsome([orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqm_, orqk_, orqh_, orqg_], is-or-resp(orqp_, (strand-map(ispace(sk-inf(orqp_))))(orqi_), orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqm_, orqk_, orqh_, orqg_)) or forsome([orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqo_, orqm_, orqk_], is-or-serv(orqp_, (strand-map(ispace(sk-inf(orqp_))))(orqi_), orqa_, orqb_, orqn_, orqo_, orqm_, orqk_))))

is-or-text

forall([ortp_, ortx_], is-or-text(ortp_, ortx_) iff ortx_ in tokens(ispace(sk-inf(ortp_))) and not(ortx_ in sk-names(ortp_)) and not(ortx_ in keys(ispace(sk-inf(ortp_)))))

is-order-iso

forall([oisf_, oisa_, oisb_], is-order-iso(oisf_, oisa_, oisb_) iff oisf_ in bijection(oisa_, oisb_) and forall([oisx_ in oisa_, oisy_ in oisa_], oisx_ < oisy_ implies oisf_(oisx_) < oisf_(oisy_)))

is-orthogonal-projection

forall([v, ks, pk], is-orthogonal-projection(v, ks, pk) iff pk in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([opz_ in vec(v)], pk(opz_) in ks and (vadd(v))(opz_, (vneg(v))(pk(opz_))) in perp(v, ks)))

is-outer-measure

forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) iff omega in set and mu in fun(power(omega), rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([a_, b_], a_ in power(omega) implies b_ in power(omega) implies a_ subset b_ implies mu(a_) <= mu(b_)) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(omega))], mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) <= esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))

is-partial-order

forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) iff grd in set and porel in set and forall([x in grd], [x, x] in porel) and forall([x in grd, y in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, x] in porel implies x = y) and forall([x in grd, y in grd, z in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, z] in porel implies [x, z] in porel))

is-partition

forall([p, n, a, b], is-partition(p, n, a, b) iff n in nn and 1 <= n and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and p in fun(nn, rr) and p(0) = a and p(n) = b and forall([i_, j_], i_ in interval(0, n) and j_ in interval(0, n) and i_ < j_ implies p(i_) < p(j_)))

is-path

forall([pgam, a, b], is-path(pgam, a, b) iff a in rr and b in rr and a < b and pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) and forall([pax_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), cc-ms, pgam, pax_)))

is-path-connected

forall([pcs_, pca_], is-path-connected(pcs_, pca_) iff is-metric-space(pcs_) and pca_ subset pts(pcs_) and forall([pcx_ in pca_, pcy_ in pca_], forsome([pcg_ in fun(ccint(0, 1), pca_)], is-continuous(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(0, 1)), pcs_, pcg_) and pcg_(0) = pcx_ and pcg_(1) = pcy_)))

is-pi-system

forall([pp], is-pi-system(pp) iff forall([pya_ in pp, pyb_ in pp], intersection(pya_, pyb_) in pp))

is-piecewise-continuous

forall([f, a, b], is-piecewise-continuous(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forsome([n_, p_], is-partition(p_, n_, a, b) and forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n_) and i_ < n_ implies forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_(i_) < t_ and t_ < p_(succ(i_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, t_)))))

is-pole

forall([mrf_, mra_, mrn_], is-pole(mrf_, mra_, mrn_) iff mra_ in cc and mrn_ in nn and 1 <= mrn_ and forsome([mrr_ in rr], 0 < mrr_ and forsome([mrg_], holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), mrg_) and not(mrg_(mra_) = 0) and forall([mrz_ in difference(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), singleton(mra_))], mrf_(mrz_) = recip(mrz_ - mra_) ^ mrn_ * mrg_(mrz_)))))

is-polish

forall([s], is-polish(s) iff is-complete(s) and is-separable(s))

is-poly-deg

forall([kk, a, f, n], is-poly-deg(kk, a, f, n) iff a subset kk and f in fun(a, kk) and forsome([pfc_ in fun(nn, kk)], forall([pfk_ in nn], succ(n) <= pfk_ implies pfc_(pfk_) = 0) and not(pfc_(n) = 0) and forall([pfz_ in a], f(pfz_) = cc-series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(pfj_, nn, pfc_(pfj_) * pfz_ ^ pfj_), succ(n)))))

is-poly-fn-on

forall([kk, a, f], is-poly-fn-on(kk, a, f) iff a subset kk and f in fun(a, kk) and forsome([pfn_ in nn, pfc_ in fun(nn, kk)], forall([pfk_ in nn], pfn_ <= pfk_ implies pfc_(pfk_) = 0) and forall([pfz_ in a], f(pfz_) = cc-series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(pfj_, nn, pfc_(pfj_) * pfz_ ^ pfj_), pfn_))))

is-positive-functional

forall([s, lam], is-positive-functional(s, lam) iff lam in fun(cont-fns(s), rr) and forall([pff_ in cont-fns(s), pfg_ in cont-fns(s)], lam(vnb-lambda(sx_, pts(s), pff_(sx_) + pfg_(sx_))) = lam(pff_) + lam(pfg_)) and forall([pfc_ in rr, pff_ in cont-fns(s)], lam(vnb-lambda(sx_, pts(s), pfc_ * pff_(sx_))) = pfc_ * lam(pff_)) and forall([pff_ in cont-fns(s)], forall([pfx_ in pts(s)], 0 <= pff_(pfx_)) implies 0 <= lam(pff_)))

is-prec-closed

forall([spc_, snd_, ssd_, srl_], is-prec-closed(spc_, snd_, ssd_, srl_) iff forall([ssx_, ssy_], [ssx_, ssy_] in ssd_ implies [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_) and forall([ssx_ in snd_, ssy_ in snd_], same-strand-before(spc_, ssx_, ssy_) implies [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_) and forall([ssx_, ssy_, ssz_], [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_ implies [ssy_, ssz_] in srl_ implies [ssx_, ssz_] in srl_))

is-premeasure

forall([omega, cls, pm], is-premeasure(omega, cls, pm) iff omega in set and cls subset power(omega) and empty-set in cls and pm in fun(cls, rr-pos-star) and pm(empty-set) = 0)

is-prime-element

forall([gps_, gpp_], is-prime-element(gps_, gpp_) iff gpp_ in carr(gps_) and not(gpp_ = zero(gps_)) and not(is-unit(gps_, gpp_)) and forall([gpa_ in carr(gps_), gpb_ in carr(gps_)], gpp_ = (mul(gps_))(gpa_, gpb_) implies is-unit(gps_, gpa_) or is-unit(gps_, gpb_)))

is-prime-nat

forall([gpn_], is-prime-nat(gpn_) iff gpn_ in nn and is-prime-element(zz-ring, gpn_))

is-prime-seq

forall([gqs_, gqp_, gqn_], is-prime-seq(gqs_, gqp_, gqn_) iff forall([gqi_ in interval(1, gqn_)], is-prime-element(gqs_, gqp_(gqi_))))

is-primitive

forall([rpg_, rpf_, rpa_, rpb_], is-primitive(rpg_, rpf_, rpa_, rpb_) iff rpa_ in rr and rpb_ in rr and rpa_ < rpb_ and rpg_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and rpf_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and is-continuous-on(rpg_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_)) and forsome([rps_], is-countable(rps_) and rps_ subset ccint(rpa_, rpb_) and forall([rpt_ in ooint(rpa_, rpb_)], not(rpt_ in rps_) implies has-deriv-at(rpg_, rpt_, rpf_(rpt_)))))

is-principal-sequence

forall([md, n, sm, es, dm], is-principal-sequence(md, n, sm, es, dm) iff es in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) and generates(md, n, es) and rel-free(md, n, es) and dm in mat(n, n, zz) and is-diagonal(zz-ring, n, n, dm) and forall([psi_ in interval(1, n)], 0 <= entry(dm, psi_, psi_)) and forall([psi_, psj_], psi_ in interval(1, n) implies psj_ in interval(1, n) implies psi_ <= psj_ implies zz-divides(entry(dm, psi_, psi_), entry(dm, psj_, psj_))) and spans(md, n, matact(md, dm, es), sm))

is-pseudometric

forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))

is-pt-open

forall([t, u], is-pt-open(t, u) iff u subset cartesian(pts(t), rr) and forall([ptw_ in u], forsome([ptv_ in opens(t)], nth(1, ptw_) in ptv_ and forsome([pte_], pos-rr(pte_) and pt-box(t, ptv_, nth(2, ptw_), pte_) subset u))))

is-ptwise-cauchy

forall([s, fam], is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps)))))

is-pw-antiderivable

forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-antiderivable(pphi_, a, b) iff forsome([paw_], is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b)))

is-pw-continuous-on

forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b) iff a in rr and b in rr and a < b and pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) and forsome([pas_ in set], card(pas_) in nn and pas_ subset ccint(a, b) and forall([pat_ in ooint(a, b)], not(pat_ in pas_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), rr-ms, pphi_, pat_))))

is-qp-digits

forall([qsp_, qta_, qtn_], is-qp-digits(qsp_, qta_, qtn_) iff qta_ in fun(zz, zz) and forall([qtk_ in zz], 0 <= qta_(qtk_) and qta_(qtk_) < qsp_ and (qtk_ < qtn_ implies qta_(qtk_) = 0)))

is-qp-expansion

forall([qsp_, qta_, qtn_, qtx_], is-qp-expansion(qsp_, qta_, qtn_, qtx_) iff is-qp-digits(qsp_, qta_, qtn_) and converges-to(qp(qsp_), vnb-lambda(qtm_, nn, qp-psum(qsp_, qta_, qtn_, qtm_)), qtx_))

is-r-net

forall([s, f, a, r], is-r-net(s, f, a, r) iff f subset a and forall([p in a], forsome([c in f], (dist(s))(c, p) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))))

is-real-hilbert-space

forall([v], is-real-hilbert-space(v) iff is-pre-hilbert-space(v) and is-complete(sip-metric-space(v)))

is-real-linear-op

forall([v, jo], is-real-linear-op(v, jo) iff jo in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], jo((vadd(v))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(v))(jo(x_), jo(y_))) and forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(v)], jo((act(v))(a_, x_)) = (act(v))(a_, jo(x_))))

is-real-measurable-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff f in fun(omega, rr) and is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, pos-part(omega, f)) and is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, neg-part(omega, f)))

is-real-orthogonal-projection

forall([v, ks, pk], is-real-orthogonal-projection(v, ks, pk) iff pk in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([sop_ in vec(v)], pk(sop_) in ks and (vadd(v))(sop_, (vneg(v))(pk(sop_))) in sip-perp(v, ks)))

is-received

forall([ssp_, ssn_], is-received(ssp_, ssn_) iff node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) = received)

is-regular-measure

forall([s, mu], is-regular-measure(s, mu) iff is-measure(pts(s), borel-sets(s), mu) and mu(pts(s)) in rr and forall([rge_ in borel-sets(s)], mu(rge_) = esup({rgv_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([rgk_ in power(pts(s))], rgk_ subset rge_ and is-compact(subspace-ms(s, rgk_)) and rgv_ = mu(rgk_))})))

is-regular-node

forall([sai_, san_], is-regular-node(sai_, san_) iff not(nth(2, san_) in adv-ids(sai_)))

is-regulated

forall([f, a, b], is-regulated(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], (x_ < b implies forsome([l_], is-right-limit(f, x_, l_))) and (a < x_ implies forsome([l_], is-left-limit(f, x_, l_)))))

is-regulated-on

forall([rpf_, rpa_, rpb_], is-regulated-on(rpf_, rpa_, rpb_) iff rpa_ in rr and rpb_ in rr and rpa_ < rpb_ and rpf_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and forall([rpx_ in ccint(rpa_, rpb_)], rpx_ < rpb_ implies forsome([rpl_], is-right-limit-within(rpf_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rpx_, rpl_))) and forall([rpx_ in ccint(rpa_, rpb_)], rpa_ < rpx_ implies forsome([rpl_], is-left-limit-within(rpf_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rpx_, rpl_))))

is-removable-singularity

forall([mrf_, mra_], is-removable-singularity(mrf_, mra_) iff mra_ in cc and forsome([mrr_ in rr], 0 < mrr_ and forsome([mrg_], holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), mrg_) and forall([mrz_ in difference(ball(cc-ms, mra_, mrr_), singleton(mra_))], mrf_(mrz_) = mrg_(mrz_)))))

is-resp-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-resp-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nln_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[received, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_, nlb_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [received, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-right-limit

forall([f, x, l], is-right-limit(f, x, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and x in rr and l in rr and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([t_], t_ in rr and x < t_ and t_ <= x + delta_ implies abs(f(t_) - l) <= eps_))))

is-right-limit-within

forall([rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_], is-right-limit-within(rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_) iff rpw_ subset rr and rpf_ in fun(rpw_, rr) and rpx_ in rr and rpl_ in rr and forall([rpe_], pos-rr(rpe_) implies forsome([rpdl_], pos-rr(rpdl_) and forall([rpt_ in rpw_], rpx_ < rpt_ implies rpt_ < rpx_ + rpdl_ implies abs(rpf_(rpt_) - rpl_) < rpe_))))

is-ring-iso

forall([rib_, ric_, rif_], is-ring-iso(rib_, ric_, rif_) iff is-hom-ring(rib_, ric_, rif_) and forall([riu_ in carr(rib_), riv_ in carr(rib_)], rif_(riu_) = rif_(riv_) implies riu_ = riv_) and forall([riw_ in carr(ric_)], forsome([riz_ in carr(rib_)], rif_(riz_) = riw_)))

is-road

forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) iff is-path(pgam, a, b) and dgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))

is-seminorm

forall([fld, m, p], is-seminorm(fld, m, p) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x in vec(m)], 0 <= p(x)) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(x, y)) <= p(x) + p(y)) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], p((act(m))(lam, x)) = (fnrm(fld))(lam) * p(x)))

is-seminorm-family

forall([fld, m, fam], is-seminorm-family(fld, m, fam) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and fam in fun(nn, fun(vec(m), rr)) and forall([k in nn], is-seminorm(fld, m, fam(k))) and forall([x in vec(m)], not(x = vzero(m)) implies forsome([k in nn], not((fam(k))(x) = 0))))

is-seminorm-family-on

forall([fld, m, ix, pf], is-seminorm-family-on(fld, m, ix, pf) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and ix in set and pf in fun(ix, fun(vec(m), rr)) and forall([sni_ in ix], is-seminorm(fld, m, pf(sni_))) and forall([snx_ in vec(m)], forall([sni_ in ix], (pf(sni_))(snx_) = 0) implies snx_ = vzero(m)))

is-seminorm-on

forall([m, s, p], is-seminorm-on(m, s, p) iff p in fun(s, rr) and forall([snx_ in s], 0 <= p(snx_)) and forall([snx_ in s, sny_ in s], p((vadd(m))(snx_, sny_)) <= p(snx_) + p(sny_)) and forall([snl_ in rr, snx_ in s], p((act(m))(snl_, snx_)) = abs(snl_) * p(snx_)))

is-sent

forall([ssp_, ssn_], is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) iff node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) = sent)

is-separable

forall([s], is-separable(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([dseq in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(n)) < eps))))

is-separate-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-separate-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sal_ in tuples(msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_)))], length(sas_) = succ(length(sal_)) and nth(1, sas_) = [received, lst(sal_)] and forall([sai_ in nn], 1 <= sai_ implies sai_ <= length(sal_) implies nth(succ(sai_), sas_) = [sent, nth(sai_, sal_)])))

is-separating-character

forall([sck_, scl_], is-separating-character(sck_, scl_) iff is-add-character(sck_, scl_) and forall([scx_ in carr(sck_)], scl_(scx_) = 1 iff scx_ in nv-integers(sck_)))

is-sesquilinear-form

forall([v, sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) iff sqf in fun(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cc) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = sqf(x_, z_) + sqf(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf((cact(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * sqf(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf(y_, x_) = conjugate(sqf(x_, y_))))

is-sieve

forall([s, sv], is-sieve(s, sv) iff sv in fun(nstar, power(pts(s))) and forall([svz_ in fun(nn, nn)], forall([sve_], pos-rr(sve_) implies forsome([svb_ in nn], forall([svn_ in nn], svb_ <= svn_ implies diam(s, sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))) <= sve_))) and forall([svn_ in nn], sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(succ(svn_)))) subset sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))) and forsome([svp_], forall([svn_ in nn], svp_ in sv(restrict(svz_, ord-segment(svn_)))))))

is-sigma-algebra

forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and omega in ca and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca))

is-sigma-finite

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-sigma-finite(omega, ca, mu) iff forsome([sff_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([sfn_ in nn], mu(sff_(sfn_)) in rr) and big-union(sfk_, nn, sff_(sfk_)) = omega))

is-sigma-union-of

forall([cb, es], is-sigma-union-of(cb, es) iff forsome([sug_ in fun(nn, cb)], es = big-union(sun_, nn, sug_(sun_))))

is-signed-measure

forall([omega, ca, nu], is-signed-measure(omega, ca, nu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and nu in fun(ca, rr-star) and nu(empty-set) = 0 and not(forsome([sma_ in ca], nu(sma_) = pos-inf) and forsome([smb_ in ca], nu(smb_) = neg-inf)) and forall([smf_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([smi_, smj_], smi_ in nn implies smj_ in nn implies not(smi_ = smj_) implies intersection(smf_(smi_), smf_(smj_)) = empty-set) implies ext-series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(smn_, nn, nu(smf_(smn_))), nu(big-union(smn_, nn, smf_(smn_))))))

is-simple-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and card(image(f, omega)) in nn and forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) in rr))

is-simple-msg

forall([sbst_, sbsk_, sbsm_], is-simple-msg(sbst_, sbsk_, sbsm_) iff not(forsome([sbsl_ in tuples(msg-set(sbst_, sbsk_))], sbsm_ = lst(sbsl_))))

is-sn-open

forall([m, ix, pf, u], is-sn-open(m, ix, pf, u) iff u subset vec(m) and forall([snx_ in u], forsome([snf_ in set], card(snf_) in nn and snf_ subset ix and forsome([sne_], pos-rr(sne_) and sn-ball(m, pf, snf_, snx_, sne_) subset u))))

is-souslin

forall([s, e], is-souslin(s, e) iff forsome([ssv_], is-sieve(s, ssv_) and souslin-set(s, ssv_) = e))

is-square-minus-id

forall([v, jo], is-square-minus-id(v, jo) iff forall([x_ in vec(v)], jo(jo(x_)) = (vneg(v))(x_)))

is-standard-borel

forall([xs, ca], is-standard-borel(xs, ca) iff forsome([sbs_], is-polish(sbs_) and forsome([sbf_ in bijection(xs, pts(sbs_))], is-measurable-map(xs, ca, pts(sbs_), borel-sets(sbs_), sbf_) and forall([sba_ in ca], image(sbf_, sba_) in borel-sets(sbs_)))))

is-step-fn

forall([f, a, b], is-step-fn(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forsome([n_, p_], is-partition(p_, n_, a, b) and forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n_) and i_ < n_ implies forsome([c_ in rr], forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_(i_) < t_ and t_ < p_(succ(i_)) implies f(t_) = c_)))))

is-step-map

forall([omega, ca, s, g], is-step-map(omega, ca, s, g) iff is-measurable-map(omega, ca, pts(s), borel-sets(s), g) and forsome([stv_ in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([stx_ in omega], forsome([stk_ in nn], g(stx_) = stv_(stk_)))))

is-strictly-below

forall([porel, x, y], is-strictly-below(porel, x, y) iff [x, y] in porel and not([y, x] in porel))

is-strictly-increasing-on

forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and s subset rr and forall([u in s, v in s], u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))

is-subfield-of-cc

forall([kk], is-subfield-of-cc(kk) iff is-subring-of-cc(kk) and forall([ansf_ in kk], not(ansf_ = 0) implies recip(ansf_) in kk))

is-subgroup

forall([sgg_, sgh_], is-subgroup(sgg_, sgh_) iff sgh_ subset carr(sgg_) and iden(sgg_) in sgh_ and forall([sga_ in sgh_, sgb_ in sgh_], (opr(sgg_))(sga_, sgb_) in sgh_) and forall([sga_ in sgh_], (inv(sgg_))(sga_) in sgh_))

is-sublinear

forall([m, p], is-sublinear(m, p) iff p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([hsx_ in vec(m), hsy_ in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(hsx_, hsy_)) <= p(hsx_) + p(hsy_)) and forall([hst_ in rr], 0 <= hst_ implies forall([hsx_ in vec(m)], p((act(m))(hst_, hsx_)) = hst_ * p(hsx_))))

is-submodule

forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) iff s subset vec(m) and vzero(m) in s and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s) and forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))

is-subnet

forall([d, x, e, y], is-subnet(d, x, e, y) iff is-directed-set(d) and is-directed-set(e) and forsome([snh_], is-cofinal-monotone(e, d, snh_) and forall([snj_ in pts(e)], y(snj_) = x(snh_(snj_)))))

is-subring-of-cc

forall([aa], is-subring-of-cc(aa) iff is-cc-subring(aa) and forall([ansn_ in aa], -ansn_ in aa))

is-subsequence

forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))

is-subspace

forall([m, s], is-subspace(m, s) iff is-submodule(m, s))

is-subterm

forall([smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_], is-subterm(smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_) iff [sma_, smb_] in subterm-rel(smt_, smk_))

is-subterm-closed

forall([smt_, smk_, smr_], is-subterm-closed(smt_, smk_, smr_) iff forall([sma_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_)], [sma_, sma_] in smr_) and forall([sma_, sml_, smi_], sml_ in tuples(msg-set(smt_, smk_)) implies smi_ in nn implies 1 <= smi_ implies smi_ <= length(sml_) implies [sma_, nth(smi_, sml_)] in smr_ implies [sma_, lst(sml_)] in smr_) and forall([sma_, smg_, smy_], smg_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_) implies smy_ in smk_ implies [sma_, smg_] in smr_ implies [sma_, encr(smg_, smy_)] in smr_))

is-summable

forall([grp, f], is-summable(grp, f) iff forsome([r in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r)))

is-symmetric-subset

forall([m, cs], is-symmetric-subset(m, cs) iff cs subset vec(m) and forall([syx_ in cs], (act(m))(-1, syx_) in cs))

is-taylor-family

forall([d, n, a, b], is-taylor-family(d, n, a, b) iff n in nn and 1 <= n and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and d(n) in fun(ooint(a, b), rr) and forall([tk_ in nn], tk_ < n implies d(tk_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) and is-continuous-on(d(tk_), ccint(a, b)) and forall([tx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(d(tk_), tx_, (d(succ(tk_)))(tx_)))))

is-tee-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-tee-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sag_], [sent, sag_], [sent, sag_]]))

is-text-trace

forall([sasp_, sap_, sas_], is-text-trace(sasp_, sap_, sas_) iff forsome([saa_ in sap_], sas_ = [[sent, saa_]]))

is-token-set

forall([smt_], is-token-set(smt_) iff smt_ in set and forall([smx_ in smt_], not(smx_ in tuples(set))))

is-top-closed

forall([t, a], is-top-closed(t, a) iff a subset pts(t) and complement-in(pts(t), a) in opens(t))

is-top-family

forall([ix, tf], is-top-family(ix, tf) iff ix in set and forall([tpi_ in ix], is-top-space(tf(tpi_))))

is-totally-bounded-in

forall([tbm_, tba_], is-totally-bounded-in(tbm_, tba_) iff forall([tbr_], pos-rr(tbr_) implies forsome([tbf_], card(tbf_) in nn and tbf_ subset pts(tbm_) and forall([tbp_ in tba_], forsome([tbc_ in tbf_], (dist(tbm_))(tbc_, tbp_) < tbr_)))))

is-tprod-open

forall([ix, tf, u], is-tprod-open(ix, tf, u) iff u subset tprod-pts(ix, tf) and forall([tpw_ in u], forsome([tpf_], tpf_ subset tprod-constr(ix, tf) and card(tpf_) in nn and tpw_ in tprod-box(ix, tf, tpf_) and tprod-box(ix, tf, tpf_) subset u)))

is-tvs

forall([fld, m, t], is-tvs(fld, m, t) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and is-hausdorff(t) and pts(t) == vec(m) and forall([tvx_, tvy_, tvu_], tvx_ in vec(m) implies tvy_ in vec(m) implies tvu_ in opens(t) implies (vadd(m))(tvx_, tvy_) in tvu_ implies forsome([tvv_ in opens(t)], tvx_ in tvv_ and forsome([tvw_ in opens(t)], tvy_ in tvw_ and forall([tva_ in tvv_, tvb_ in tvw_], (vadd(m))(tva_, tvb_) in tvu_)))) and forall([tvr_, tvx_, tvu_], tvr_ in carr(scal(m)) implies tvx_ in vec(m) implies tvu_ in opens(t) implies (act(m))(tvr_, tvx_) in tvu_ implies forsome([tvd_], pos-rr(tvd_) and forsome([tvw_ in opens(t)], tvx_ in tvw_ and forall([tvs_ in carr(scal(m))], (fnrm(fld))((add(scal(m)))(tvs_, (neg(scal(m)))(tvr_))) < tvd_ implies forall([tva_ in tvw_], (act(m))(tvs_, tva_) in tvu_))))))

is-ufd

forall([gus_], is-ufd(gus_) iff is-integral-domain(gus_) and forall([gua_ in carr(gus_)], not(gua_ = zero(gus_)) implies forsome([guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) and forsome([gun_ in nn, gup_], is-prime-seq(gus_, gup_, gun_) and gua_ = (mul(gus_))(guu_, prod-ring(gus_, gup_, interval(1, gun_)))))) and forall([gun_ in nn, gup_], is-prime-seq(gus_, gup_, gun_) implies forall([gum_ in nn, guq_], is-prime-seq(gus_, guq_, gum_) implies forall([guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) implies forall([guv_], is-unit(gus_, guv_) implies (mul(gus_))(guu_, prod-ring(gus_, gup_, interval(1, gun_))) = (mul(gus_))(guv_, prod-ring(gus_, guq_, interval(1, gum_))) implies gun_ = gum_ and forsome([gus2_ in bijection(interval(1, gun_), interval(1, gum_))], forall([gui_ in interval(1, gun_)], associated(gus_, gup_(gui_), guq_(gus2_(gui_))))))))))

is-ultrametric

forall([ums_], is-ultrametric(ums_) iff is-metric-space(ums_) and forall([umx_ in pts(ums_), umy_ in pts(ums_), umz_ in pts(ums_)], (dist(ums_))(umx_, umy_) <= max((dist(ums_))(umx_, umz_), (dist(ums_))(umz_, umy_))))

is-unif-cauchy

forall([s, fam], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps))))))

is-unif-limit-on

forall([fam, g, a, b], is-unif-limit-on(fam, g, a, b) iff fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)) and g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_], k_ in nn and n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(g(x_) - (fam(k_))(x_)) <= eps_)))))

is-uniformly-continuous

forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))

is-unit

forall([gus_, guu_], is-unit(gus_, guu_) iff guu_ in carr(gus_) and forsome([guv_ in carr(gus_)], (mul(gus_))(guu_, guv_) = one(gus_)))

is-upper-bound

forall([grd, porel, ch, b], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch, b) iff b in grd and forall([x in ch], [x, b] in porel))

is-upper-triangular

forall([a, n, d], is-upper-triangular(a, n, d) iff forall([ltu_, ltv_], ltu_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ < ltu_ implies entry(d, ltu_, ltv_) = zero(a)))

little-o-at

forall([g, a], little-o-at(g, a) iff g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and forsome([eps in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, eps, a) and eps(a) = 0 and forall([x in rr], g(x) = eps(x) * (x - a))))

mat-equiv

forall([a, m, n, c, d], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) iff forsome([u], is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) and forsome([v], is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) and d = matmul(a, matmul(a, u, c), v))))

meromorphic-on

forall([mru_, mrf_], meromorphic-on(mru_, mrf_) iff is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), mru_) and forsome([mrs_], mrs_ subset mru_ and holomorphic-on(difference(mru_, mrs_), mrf_) and forall([mrx_ in mrs_], is-removable-singularity(mrf_, mrx_) or forsome([mrn_], is-pole(mrf_, mrx_, mrn_)))))

net-converges-to

forall([t, d, x, a], net-converges-to(t, d, x, a) iff is-top-space(t) and is-directed-set(d) and x in fun(pts(d), pts(t)) and a in pts(t) and forall([ncu_ in opens(t)], a in ncu_ implies is-eventually-in(d, x, ncu_)))

net-unif-conv-on

forall([d, p, f, k], net-unif-conv-on(d, p, f, k) iff forall([uce_], pos-rr(uce_) implies forsome([uci_ in pts(d)], forall([ucj_ in pts(d)], [uci_, ucj_] in dleq(d) implies forall([ucy_ in k], abs((p(ucj_))(ucy_) - f(ucy_)) < uce_)))))

npe

forall([m, s, f, t, g], npe(m, s, f, t, g) iff is-submodule(m, t) and s subset t and is-linear-functional-on(m, t, g) and extends-on(s, g, f) and forall([w_ in t], abs(g(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_)))

null-homotopic

forall([hmu_, hmg_, hma_, hmb_], null-homotopic(hmu_, hmg_, hma_, hmb_) iff hmg_(hmb_) = hmg_(hma_) and homotopic(hmu_, hmg_, vnb-lambda(hmx_, ccint(hma_, hmb_), hmg_(hma_)), hma_, hmb_))

null-rr-seq

forall([rad], null-rr-seq(rad) iff rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([k], k in nn and n <= k implies rad(k) <= eps))))

occurs-in-space

forall([ssp_, ssu_], occurs-in-space(ssp_, ssu_) iff forsome([ssn_ in nodes(ssp_)], is-subterm(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_), ssu_, node-content(ssp_, ssn_))))

odd

forall([x], odd(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k + 1))

opg-rep

forall([rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rpt_, rpy_], opg-rep(rpg_, rpa_, rpp_, rpt_, rpy_) iff 0 <= rpt_ and forsome([rpn_ in nn, rpr_ in rr], 0 <= rpr_ and rpr_ < rpa_ and rpt_ = rpn_ * rpa_ + rpr_ and rpy_ = (opr(rpg_))(gpow(rpg_, rpp_(rpa_), rpn_), rpp_(rpr_))) or rpt_ < 0 and forsome([rpn_ in nn, rpr_ in rr], 0 <= rpr_ and rpr_ < rpa_ and 0 - rpt_ = rpn_ * rpa_ + rpr_ and rpy_ = (inv(rpg_))((opr(rpg_))(gpow(rpg_, rpp_(rpa_), rpn_), rpp_(rpr_)))))

originates

forall([ssp_, ssu_, ssn_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssn_) iff is-entry-point(ssp_, ssn_, {ssw_ in msg-set(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_)): is-subterm(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_), ssu_, ssw_)}))

padic-frac-rep

forall([cpp_, cpx_, cpr_], padic-frac-rep(cpp_, cpx_, cpr_) iff cpr_ in qq and forsome([cpz_ in zz, cpm_ in nn], cpr_ * cpp_ ^ cpm_ = cpz_) and (padic-abs(cpp_))(cpx_ + -cpr_) <= 1)

padic-rel

forall([cpp_, cpx_, cpv_], padic-rel(cpp_, cpx_, cpv_) iff cpv_ in rr and (cpx_ = 0 and cpv_ = 0 or not(cpx_ = 0) and forsome([cpa_ in zz, cpb_ in zz], not(cpb_ = 0) and cpx_ * cpb_ = cpa_ and forsome([cpi_ in nn, cpj_ in nn], zz-ord-is(cpp_, cpa_, cpi_) and zz-ord-is(cpp_, cpb_, cpj_) and cpv_ * 2 ^ cpi_ = 2 ^ cpj_))))

pointwise-bounded

forall([s, fam], pointwise-bounded(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s)], forsome([bd in rr], forall([k in nn], abs((fam(k))(x)) <= bd))))

pos-rr

forall([r], pos-rr(r) iff r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r))

positively-oriented

forall([poa_, pob_, poc_], positively-oriented(poa_, pob_, poc_) iff forall([pou_ in interior(cc-ms, conv3(poa_, pob_, poc_))], angle(pob_ - pou_, poa_ - pou_) < pi and angle(poc_ - pou_, pob_ - pou_) < pi and angle(poa_ - pou_, poc_ - pou_) < pi))

pred-code

forall([mcpk_], pred-code(mcpk_) iff mcpk_ = 7 or mcpk_ = 9 or mcpk_ = 11 or mcpk_ = 13)

prod-abs-converges

prod-converges-to

forall([pcf_, pcl_], prod-converges-to(pcf_, pcl_) iff pcf_ in fun(nn, cc) and forsome([pcm_ in nn], forall([pck_ in nn], pcm_ <= pck_ implies not(pcf_(pck_) = 0)) and forsome([pcz_ in cc], not(pcz_ = 0) and converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(pcn_, nn, pprod(vnb-lambda(pcj_, nn, pcf_(pcm_ + pcj_)), pcn_)), pcz_) and pcl_ = pprod(pcf_, pcm_) * pcz_)))
